# Processed American cheese

<section class="hmi-quick-read" id="processed-american-cheese-quick-read" data-hmi-quick-read aria-labelledby="processed-american-cheese-quick-read-label"><div class="hmi-quick-read-label" id="processed-american-cheese-quick-read-label">Quick read</div>

Processed American cheese in the HMI library has 83 reported results for lead, cadmium, total arsenic, inorganic arsenic, total mercury, nickel, chromium, aluminium, tin, antimony, uranium, Co, Cu, Fe, Mn, Zn, V, Se, Ba, Hg, Sr, Ti and Tl from 7 sources.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/abulgasim2025-sabratha-processed-cheese-lead-cadmium|4]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Abulgasim AM, Elbakosh EH · 2025</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/abulgasim2025-sabratha-processed-cheese-lead-cadmium">Assessment Of Milk Fat Content And Certain Heavy Metals In Various Cheese Types ( W. Libya).</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Libyan Journal of Medical Research 19(1):30-34</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/abulgasim2025-sabratha-processed-cheese-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/al-shdidi2010-baghdad-processed-cheese-emulsifying-salts|5]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Al-Shdidi AMS · 2010</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/al-shdidi2010-baghdad-processed-cheese-emulsifying-salts">Study of Pollution Levels of heavy metals residues in the locally produced cheese &amp; emulsifying salts</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">The Iraqi Journal of Veterinary Medicine 34(2):193-198</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/al-shdidi2010-baghdad-processed-cheese-emulsifying-salts">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fera2015-uk-tds-metals|6]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Baxter M, Brereton N · 2015</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fera2015-uk-tds-metals">Total Diet Study of metals and other elements in food</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food and Environment Research Agency report for the UK Food Standards Agency, Fera report 15/06, project FS102081</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fera2015-uk-tds-metals">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

<ul>
<li>
<strong>Metals measured:</strong>

Lead, cadmium, total arsenic, inorganic arsenic, total mercury and nickel and others, in 83 results from 7 sources.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/abulgasim2025-sabratha-processed-cheese-lead-cadmium|4]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Abulgasim AM, Elbakosh EH · 2025</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/abulgasim2025-sabratha-processed-cheese-lead-cadmium">Assessment Of Milk Fat Content And Certain Heavy Metals In Various Cheese Types ( W. Libya).</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Libyan Journal of Medical Research 19(1):30-34</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/abulgasim2025-sabratha-processed-cheese-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</li>
<li>
<strong>Main drivers:</strong>

Form, basis and where the processed American cheese was sampled; reporting limits decide how many results are non-detects.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/abulgasim2025-sabratha-processed-cheese-lead-cadmium|4]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Abulgasim AM, Elbakosh EH · 2025</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/abulgasim2025-sabratha-processed-cheese-lead-cadmium">Assessment Of Milk Fat Content And Certain Heavy Metals In Various Cheese Types ( W. Libya).</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Libyan Journal of Medical Research 19(1):30-34</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/abulgasim2025-sabratha-processed-cheese-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</li>
<li>
<strong>Practical framing:</strong>

Ask for lot results with a quantification limit well below the limit that applies in the destination market.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/abulgasim2025-sabratha-processed-cheese-lead-cadmium|4]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Abulgasim AM, Elbakosh EH · 2025</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/abulgasim2025-sabratha-processed-cheese-lead-cadmium">Assessment Of Milk Fat Content And Certain Heavy Metals In Various Cheese Types ( W. Libya).</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Libyan Journal of Medical Research 19(1):30-34</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/abulgasim2025-sabratha-processed-cheese-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</li>

<a id="decision-summary"></a>
## Processed American cheese overview

<a id="hmi-ing-004"></a>
<a id="which-contamination-concerns-are-supported-for-this-ingredient-and-its-defined-forms"></a>
<a id="hmi-ing-005"></a>
<a id="which-sourcing-or-production-decisions-deserve-priority-on-the-available-evidence"></a>
<a id="hmi-ing-006"></a>
<a id="which-evidence-gaps-most-limit-those-decisions"></a>

Processed American cheese in the HMI library has 83 reported results for lead, cadmium, total arsenic, inorganic arsenic, total mercury, nickel, chromium, aluminium, tin, antimony, uranium, Co, Cu, Fe, Mn, Zn, V, Se, Ba, Hg, Sr, Ti and Tl from 7 sources.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/abulgasim2025-sabratha-processed-cheese-lead-cadmium|4]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Abulgasim AM, Elbakosh EH · 2025</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/abulgasim2025-sabratha-processed-cheese-lead-cadmium">Assessment Of Milk Fat Content And Certain Heavy Metals In Various Cheese Types ( W. Libya).</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Libyan Journal of Medical Research 19(1):30-34</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/abulgasim2025-sabratha-processed-cheese-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/al-shdidi2010-baghdad-processed-cheese-emulsifying-salts|5]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Al-Shdidi AMS · 2010</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/al-shdidi2010-baghdad-processed-cheese-emulsifying-salts">Study of Pollution Levels of heavy metals residues in the locally produced cheese &amp; emulsifying salts</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">The Iraqi Journal of Veterinary Medicine 34(2):193-198</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/al-shdidi2010-baghdad-processed-cheese-emulsifying-salts">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fera2015-uk-tds-metals|6]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Baxter M, Brereton N · 2015</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fera2015-uk-tds-metals">Total Diet Study of metals and other elements in food</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food and Environment Research Agency report for the UK Food Standards Agency, Fera report 15/06, project FS102081</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fera2015-uk-tds-metals">Read source →</a></span></span></sup> WHO GEMS/Food submissions reported lead in processed American cheese, as sold from 302 results in 2 reporting regions, with subgroup medians below 50 µg/kg, mostly non-detects.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup> In the US Total Diet Study, processed American cheese, as sold had a median lead result below 4 µg/kg across 3 composites from 2018–2020.<sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

Spungen et al. 2024 reported lead at 3.4 µg/kg (mean) and cadmium at 2.8 µg/kg (mean) in processed American cheese, as sold from all samples.<sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup> Crupi et al. 2022 reported total arsenic at 1 µg/kg (mean ± 1 SD) in processed American cheese, as sold from retail, markets in Sicily.<sup class="cite">[[sources/crupi2022-sicily-lactose-free-dairy-elements|7]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Crupi R, Lo Turco V, Gugliandolo E, Nava V, Potortì AG, Cuzzocrea S, Di Bella G, Licata P · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/crupi2022-sicily-lactose-free-dairy-elements">Mineral Composition in Delactosed Dairy Products: Quality and Safety Status</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Foods 2022, 11, 139</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/crupi2022-sicily-lactose-free-dairy-elements">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

For a buyer the practical points are the form bought, the basis a result is reported on, where the processed American cheese came from and the laboratory’s reporting limit. Testing finds contamination but does not remove it.

<div class="hmi-analyte-explorer" id="hmi-occurrence-processed-american-cheese" data-analyte-explorer data-compact-intro="true" data-source-summaries="true"><details class="hmi-drawer-disclosure" data-explorer-context><summary data-open-context aria-haspopup="dialog" aria-controls="hmi-occurrence-processed-american-cheese-drawer">Comparison methods and scope</summary><div data-explorer-context-content>

<p>Each row is one statistic a source reports for one form of processed american cheese, group and metal; percentiles, ranges and maxima reported for the same samples as a mean or median are shown on that row. Different forms and preparation states are kept apart, and dry-weight results never share a reference with as-sold or fresh-weight results. Ratings place a result against an HMI reference built from monitoring results and ordinary-setting medians for the same form and basis. Same-study contrasts compare a contaminated or treated site with its control, targeted with random sampling, a prepared form with the unprocessed one, or one group of a study with the median of its other groups. Held rows failed a check against their own source and are shown but not compared.</p>

Processed American cheese, as sold; As sold, as eaten or fresh weight, µg/kg; no moisture conversion. Distribution of subgroup median concentration values.</p><div class="hmi-drawer-table"><table><thead><tr><th scope="col">Reference (µg/kg)</th><th scope="col">Q1</th><th scope="col">Median</th><th scope="col">Q3</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Primary HMI reference</td><td>≤4</td><td>≤6.3</td><td>6.3–19 (bounded)</td></tr><tr><td>Alternative weighting</td><td>≤4</td><td>≤6.3</td><td>6.3–20 (bounded)</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p>37 reference units; 2 sampling families. Counts describe evidence units, not independent studies or certainty.</p><div class="hmi-drawer-table"><table><thead><tr><th scope="col">Contributing family</th><th scope="col">Reference units</th><th scope="col">Weight</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>WHO GEMS/Food</td><td>34</td><td>50%</td></tr><tr><td>FDA Total Diet Study</td><td>3</td><td>50%</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Methods</p><p>HMI pools subgroup medians for processed american cheese, as sold on a as sold, as eaten or fresh weight basis: monitoring results summarized as one median per reporting region, sampling type and collection year, total diet composites and printed sample values as single observations, and printed subgroup medians from ordinary farms or markets. Each sampling family has equal total weight. Non-detects stay as zero-to-limit intervals, so quartiles carry lower and upper bounds. Means, ranges and maxima never enter as observations. Other forms, other bases, targeted sampling and results from mining, industrial, wastewater-affected, experimental, fertiliser or manufacturer settings are kept out of this reference.</p><p>Inverse weighted empirical CDF; midpoint at exact cumulative-weight boundaries. At least four reference units and a definitely positive Q1 to Q3 spread are required. This is a display eligibility rule, not statistical validation.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Limitations</p><p>2 sampling families with different designs; none is a probability sample of world supply. Reporting regions and purchase markets are not growing areas. Non-detects with differing limits widen the bounds. A position is descriptive concentration, not a safety or compliance judgment.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Admitted source families</p><ul><li><a href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants" class="internal" data-slug="sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants" data-no-cite-meta>WHO GEMS/Food</a></li><li><a href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020" class="internal" data-slug="sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020" data-no-cite-meta>FDA Total Diet Study</a></li></ul><details class="hmi-drawer-disclosure"><summary>Reference version and interpretation</summary><p>Frozen evidence selection: 2026-10-22. HMI median ≤6.3; Q1 ≤4; Q3 6.3–19 (bounded) µg/kg. 2 families. Reporting bounds prevent a rating.</p><p>Reporting bounds or insufficient reference spread prevent a stable Low / Moderate / High classification.</p><p>This finding’s sampling family contributes to the fixed reference.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Sensitivity to weighting</p><p>Equal weight per subgroup: Limited data. The position remains unresolved under this alternative. This check does not address all sampling, rounding or analytical uncertainty.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Sensitivity to source selection</p><ul><li>Without WHO GEMS/Food: Limited data.</li><li>Without FDA Total Diet Study: Limited data.</li></ul><p>These checks show dependence on each sampling family; they are not confidence intervals.</p><div data-baseline-reference="hmi-pooled-reference-v2:processed-american-cheese-9bf5e5c2c50a:f240755ab9af"><a href="#hmi-reference-hmi-pooled-reference-v2:processed-american-cheese-9bf5e5c2c50a:f240755ab9af">Reference, contributing evidence and methods</a></div></div><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Collection period</p><p data-timeline-scope>2004–2017</p><div data-selected-timeline><p>No year-by-year sample register is reconstructed from this summary.</p></div><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Geographic scope</p><p data-regional-scope>EURO</p><div data-selected-regional><p>Purchase or reporting market; growing area not identified.</p></div><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Sampling, analysis and reporting limits</p><p>As described by the monitoring programme.</p><p>Non-detects are intervals from zero to the reporting limit; never a measured zero</p><p></p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Shared samples and study overlap</p><p>Subgroup rows from the same source share its laboratory and sampling frame; they are not independent studies.</p><p>Each table row describes a food/analyte result. Metals do not move together, so a lot that passes a lead limit says little about cadmium, arsenic or nickel unless those are tested as well.

References identify the sources used in these answers. The Sources index preserves additional discovery links; inclusion there does not mean a source has been reviewed for this assessment. Corrections, completed searches, new direct evidence and changes in requirements should reopen the relevant answer.


<a id="universal-faqs"></a>
## Processed American cheese FAQs

<details class="qa" data-hmi-faq="1" id="what-heavy-metals-are-found-in-processed-american-cheese" data-anchor="what-heavy-metals-are-found-in-processed-american-cheese" data-question="What heavy metals are found in processed American cheese?">
<summary><span class="mk" aria-hidden="true"></span>What heavy metals are found in processed American cheese?</summary>
<div class="ans" data-hmi-faq-answer="1">

The library reports lead, cadmium, total arsenic, inorganic arsenic, total mercury, nickel, chromium, aluminium, tin, antimony, uranium, Co, Cu, Fe, Mn, Zn, V, Se, Ba, Hg, Sr, Ti and Tl in processed American cheese, most often lead, cadmium and total arsenic.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/abulgasim2025-sabratha-processed-cheese-lead-cadmium|4]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Abulgasim AM, Elbakosh EH · 2025</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/abulgasim2025-sabratha-processed-cheese-lead-cadmium">Assessment Of Milk Fat Content And Certain Heavy Metals In Various Cheese Types ( W. Libya).</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Libyan Journal of Medical Research 19(1):30-34</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/abulgasim2025-sabratha-processed-cheese-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup> WHO GEMS/Food submissions reported lead in processed American cheese, as sold from 302 results in 2 reporting regions, with subgroup medians below 50 µg/kg, mostly non-detects.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</div>
</details>

<details class="qa" data-hmi-faq="1" id="how-much-of-the-arsenic-in-processed-american-cheese-is-inorganic" data-anchor="how-much-of-the-arsenic-in-processed-american-cheese-is-inorganic" data-question="How much of the arsenic in processed American cheese is inorganic?">
<summary><span class="mk" aria-hidden="true"></span>How much of the arsenic in processed American cheese is inorganic?</summary>
<div class="ans" data-hmi-faq-answer="1">

Inorganic arsenic, the more toxic form, was measured in 1 result from 1 source, far fewer than total arsenic.<sup class="cite">[[sources/fera2015-uk-tds-metals|6]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Baxter M, Brereton N · 2015</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fera2015-uk-tds-metals">Total Diet Study of metals and other elements in food</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food and Environment Research Agency report for the UK Food Standards Agency, Fera report 15/06, project FS102081</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fera2015-uk-tds-metals">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</div>
</details>
